This approach is instantiated within deterministic, probabilistic, and LLM-based methods, enabling a comparative analysis of their strengths and limitations for text-based spatial inference. On a pseudo-NGP benchmark, probabilistic inference achieves the highest accuracy (median error 1.43 km; A@1 km 36%), while the LLM yields competitive but less precise estimates (median error 1.80 km; A@1 km 31%), indicating that, despite advances in LLMs, traditional modelling remains advantageous when high spatial precision is required.

</details>


### [101] [Calibrating WEAT Against Anisotropy: ZCA Whitening as a Geometric Pre-Processing Step for Embedding Association Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06908)
*Seitaro Ono,Senna Ross,Jun Saiki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将ZCA白化作为词嵌入关联测试（WEAT）的几何预处理步骤，通过在七个模型和十个标准测试套件上验证，发现该变换能显著降低嵌入空间的各向异性，并使超过30%的WEAT结果改变显著性状态，表明未校准的偏差测量可能不准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WEAT依赖余弦相似度衡量语义关联，但其前提是嵌入空间近似各向同性。然而许多广泛使用的语言模型并不满足这一假设，导致偏差测量结果的可靠性存疑。

Method: 将ZCA白化作为WEAT的预处理步骤，通过将嵌入空间的协方差变换为单位矩阵来恢复各向同性条件，同时最小化对原始向量的扰动。在10个标准WEAT测试套件和7个覆盖三种架构家族的模型上进行评估，共计70个模型-任务组合。

Result: ZCA白化在所有模型上显著降低了嵌入空间的各向异性，对高各向异性模型还能提升标准语义相似性基准的表现。校准后超过30%的WEAT结果改变了显著性状态，效应量随偏差类别不同而双向移动。

Conclusion: 未校准的各向异性嵌入空间中的偏差测量可能同时存在高估和低估，以往报告的结果应谨慎解读，并有必要使用校准方法重新评估。

Abstract: We propose Zero-phase Component Analysis (ZCA) whitening as a geometric pre-processing step for the Word Embedding Association Test (WEAT). WEAT is a bias measurement method widely used in both computational social science and AI fairness research. It relies on cosine similarity as a measure of semantic association, which assumes that the embedding space is approximately isotropic. However, prior work has reported that many widely used language models do not satisfy this assumption, raising concerns about the reliability of bias measurements. ZCA whitening transforms the covariance of the embedding space into the identity matrix while minimizing perturbation to the original vectors. This transformation restores the isotropy condition on which WEAT relies. We evaluate our approach on ten standard WEAT test suites and seven models spanning three architectural families, yielding 70 model-task combinations. The results show that ZCA whitening substantially reduces the anisotropy of the embedding spaces across all models. Particularly for highly anisotropic models, we further observe improvements on standard semantic similarity benchmarks, indicating that the calibrated space better captures semantic associations. After calibration, over 30% of WEAT results change significance status, and effect sizes shift in both directions depending on bias category. These shifts suggest that uncalibrated measurements may both overestimate and underestimate the associations encoded in the embedding space. These findings indicate that previously reported bias measurements in anisotropic embedding spaces should be interpreted with caution and may benefit from re-evaluation with calibrated methods. Our approach contributes to restoring the measurement foundation of WEAT across both computational social science and AI fairness research.

</details>


### [102] [Ask-E: An Environment for Calibrated Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06933)
*Sarah Pratt,Jae Sung Park,Scott Geng,Ali Farhadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Ask-E环境，用于基准测试和训练模型根据给定技能水平编写问题（而非解答问题）的能力。通过两个语言模型的能力边界定义目标技能水平，生成的问题若恰好只有一个模型能解决则视为校准成功。实验发现即使是前沿模型在校准基准上准确率也低于50%，而在此环境上训练能提升多个下游数学基准的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成与模型当前能力前沿精准对齐的问题是困难且耗时的任务，需要超越模型自身能力。当模型不断进步时，这一约束变得越来越沉重。作者的关键洞察是，能够为给定能力前沿生成校准问题这一事实本身就意味着模型具备超越该前沿的能力，因此可以将此转化为训练和评测模型的有用信号。

Method: 提出Ask-E环境，用两个现成语言模型的能力范围定义目标技能水平（skill levels）。生成问题被视为校准成功当且仅当两个模型中恰好一个能解决问题，从而将问题精确置于目标范围内。Ask-E同时作为基准和训练环境，让模型为多种技能水平生成校准问题。

Result: 即使是最前沿的模型在该校准基准上准确率也低于50%，说明仍有较大提升空间。在此环境中训练模型，即使不引入新数学数据、不与更强模型交互、不使用正确性奖励，也能在多个下游数学基准上取得改进。

Conclusion: Ask-E验证了"生成校准问题意味着超越目标能力"的核心假设，并证明基于问题生成而非问题解答的训练范式可以有效提升模型的数学能力，为未来模型能力评测和训练提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Today, we improve models by training and evaluating them on problems at the frontier of their abilities. Creating such problems is itself a demanding task, requiring the ability to probe model limits and generalize beyond existing question distributions. It also means placing problems at a precise difficulty level, which requires understanding what it takes to solve them. In short, generating problems calibrated to a model's current frontier demands capability beyond it, an increasingly burdensome constraint as models improve. Our key insight is that we can leverage this constraint to our advantage: a model that can generate problems consistently calibrated to a given frontier must possess capability beyond it. Accordingly, we present Ask-E, an environment that benchmarks and trains models on their ability to write questions at a given skill level, rather than answer them. Concretely, we define target skill levels as ranges bounded by the capabilities of two existing language models. A generated question is successfully calibrated if exactly one of the two models can solve it, placing it precisely within the target range and differentiating the capabilities of these models. Ask-E serves both as a benchmark and a training environment, where models generate problems calibrated to a variety of skill levels. We find that even frontier models achieve below 50% calibration on the benchmark, leaving significant headroom to measure future progress. We also show that training on this environment leads to improvements across a number of downstream math benchmarks even with no new math data, no interaction with stronger models, and no correctness-based reward.

</details>


### [103] [Explicit, Not Longer: What Makes Epistemic Stance Survive Memory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06953)
*Alex Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现：在智能体记忆压缩中，将论断立场写为独立标注字段相比括号附注能使保留率提升约15个点（两个模型上均有显著统计差异，p=0.00005），并在Haiku上的预注册复制中得到验证（+15.6点）。但消融实验显示单个模型各自支持不同的机制解释，作者仅宣称交集：让立场显式化而非单纯加长，且最有效的显式化方式依赖于具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆压缩系统倾向于丢弃修饰性限定词，导致论断的认识论立场（epistemic standing）在写入记忆后往往无法保留。作者希望找出什么样的条件决定了立场能否在压缩记忆中幸存。

Method: 设计配对笔记实验：两份笔记包含相同论断与相同立场，仅立场位置不同（标注字段 vs 括号附注），同预算同填充笔记下由两个模型压缩，盲读者评分。含预注册复制（Haiku）、消融实验（格式/长度/句法）、确定性读取器对照、五十分人工标注。

Result: 标注字段方式提升保留约15点（模型一37 vs 2，模型二30 vs 8，置换检验p=0.00005）；Haiku预注册复制+15.6点（38 vs 1）。消融显示标签在两模型均有帮助，长度均无帮助，完整句式仅对一模型有效。确定性读取器复现部分结果但无法复现长度和标签效果。

Conclusion: 核心结论是让立场显式化而非仅仅加长文本；但不同的模型受益于不同的显式化方式，因此不能声称单一普适机制。作者以极强透明性报告了所有分歧案例（含手标注的7处不一致和9条撤回声明），体现严格自我审查的方法论态度。

Abstract: Agent memory systems compress what they store, and compression is built to drop qualifiers, so a claim's epistemic standing tends not to survive being written to memory. We ask what governs whether it does. Matched notes carry the identical claim and identical stance and differ only in where that stance sits; one model compresses both under the same budget among the same filler notes, and a blind reader that never sees the condition scores the result. Across 60 claims in seven registers, writing the stance as a labelled field rather than a bracketed aside raises retention by about 15 points on two models (37 claims to 2 on one, 30 to 8 on the other; permutation p=0.00005), and a pre-registered replication on Haiku, its prediction and decision rule committed before the run, gives +15.6 points, 38 claims to 1. Ablating the format on both models gives the same net effect from different parts: labels help on both (+9.7 and +12.8) and length helps on neither, but wording the stance as a full sentence is the largest component on one model (+12.5) and worth nothing on the other (+0.6). Either model alone would have licensed a confident and different mechanism, so we claim only the intersection: make the stance explicit, not merely longer, and expect the best way of being explicit to depend on the model. A deterministic readout with no model reproduces the two-cell direction and five of seven ablation contrasts, but not length or labels, which we therefore do not claim on one instrument. Fifty hand labels (kappa=0.75) agree on direction; we print their seven disagreements in full. We also report nine withdrawn claims, three of them former title claims of this paper.

</details>


### [104] [Can Language Models Imagine Without Seeing? Ekphrasis: Measuring Visual Creative Ideation in Text-Only LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06967)
*Hongyu Luo,He Wang,Huihao Jing,Hong Ting Tsang,Yuxuan Liu,Wuganjing Song,Yauwai Yim,Chunyang Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉创造性思维（VCI）概念，即纯文本语言模型在图像生成之前产生有用、富有表现力且具备群体新颖性的文本视觉规划的能力，并引入Ekphrasis基准——一个包含400个任务、跨越抽象、组合、变换和适应的评估框架。实验表明VCI能将有用性、表现力和新颖性三个维度分离评估，且文本层面的VCI排序在图像渲染后依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估无法区分文本语言模型是否真正具备视觉概念原创能力，因为流畅的视觉描述可能掩盖视觉规划失败——看似有创意实则重复常见视觉陈词滥调或无法指定可渲染的场景。

Method: 提出Ekphrasis基准，包含400个任务覆盖抽象、组合、变换和适应四类。采用匿名成对比较与维度特定检查表评分，通过Bradley-Terry模型聚合偏好，并使用Typed Idea Graphs将任务特定群体陈词滥调转化为新颖性参考。在14个语言模型上进行评估，并进行跨模态接地研究验证。

Result: VCI能将有用性、表现力和新颖性三个维度分离而非简化为流畅性：强模型通过不同画像达到相似总体分数，有用的规划仍可能视觉上陈词滥调。跨模态研究表明文本级VCI排序在忠实渲染和盲图像级偏好判断后基本保持。

Conclusion: Ekphrasis可作为衡量超越散文质量的视觉思维能力的有效工具，表明VCI不仅能独立于流畅性评估视觉创意，且其排序在图像生成后依然稳健。

Abstract: Current evaluations do not isolate whether text-only language models can originate visual concepts before image generation. Fluent visual prose can hide visual-plan failures: an answer may appear creative while repeating familiar visual clichés or failing to specify a renderable scene. We define Visual Creative Ideation (VCI) as the ability to produce textual visual plans that are useful, expressive, and population-novel, and introduce Ekphrasis, a 400-task benchmark spanning Abstraction, Combination, Transformation, and Adaptation. Ekphrasis scores anonymized pairwise comparisons with dimension-specific checklists, aggregates preferences with Bradley-Terry models, and uses Typed Idea Graphs to convert task-specific population clichés into novelty references. Across 14 language models, VCI separates usefulness, expressiveness, and novelty rather than reducing to fluency: strong models achieve similar overall scores through different profiles, and useful plans can remain visually clichéd. A cross-modal grounding study further shows that text-level VCI ordering largely survives faithful rendering and blind image-level preference judgment, supporting Ekphrasis as a measure of visual ideation beyond prose quality.

</details>


### [105] [PHASE-Tree: Modeling Character-State Evolution in Long-Horizon Role-Playing Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06975)
*Bo Tang,Jianan Yang,Junyi Zhu,Yiquan Wu,Rui Zhao,Zhengyu Yang,Yang Zhang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li,Jiajun Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PHASE-Tree多时间尺度角色状态树用于长程角色扮演，解决静态画像无法局部更新和基准缺乏演化状态测试两大问题；同时引入LongEvoRoleBench基准，在长对话核心上文本版PHASE-Tree在11/12个数据-指标单元格中排名第一，相较于外部文本基线分别提升19.7%、12.4%和15.1%的角色级、语义和嵌入分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色扮演工作存在两个不足：一是角色表示通常是静态画像，无法在不破坏未变化特质的情况下进行局部更新；二是现有基准主要测试人物形象保持和记忆回忆，而非测试模型是否能从角色当前演化状态出发进行对话生成。

Method: 提出PHASE-Tree，一个多时间尺度角色状态树，包含不可变的身份根节点和可变的性格、会话、时刻三个层次，每个可变字段可作为局部更新（情节内和跨情节）的可寻址目标；可通过显式文本提供或隐式参数适应来条件化生成。同时构建LongEvoRoleBench基准，将四个长对话语料用于跨情节演化测试，四个短对话语料用于场景内状态追踪检查，统一采用下一句话生成协议。

Result: 在长对话核心任务中，文本版PHASE-Tree在12个数据-指标单元格中11个排名第一（对比内部变体），且在对所有外部文本基线中12个单元格全部最优，角色级、语义和嵌入分数分别提升19.7%、12.4%和15.1%。盲测200份回复中，人类评分与GPT-4.1评判相关（Pearson r=0.65）；在n=10的描述性子集上Overall差异为+0.20。长对话语义优势在不同LLM评判和生成骨干网络中均保持一致。

Conclusion: PHASE-Tree通过分层、可寻址的角色状态表示有效解决了长程角色扮演中角色演化状态生成的问题，LongEvoRoleBench为该任务提供了更合理的评测基准，实验结果验证了该方法在角色一致性保持和演化状态表达上的优势。

Abstract: Long-horizon role-playing demands that characters remain recognizable as they evolve with the narrative. Yet existing work falls short on two fronts: representations are typically static profiles that cannot be updated locally without destabilizing unchanged traits, and benchmarks mainly test persona preservation and memory recall rather than whether a model speaks from a character's currently evolved state. We address both. PHASE-Tree is a multi-timescale character-state tree with an immutable identity root and mutable persona, session, and moment layers, making each mutable field an addressable target for localized within- and cross-episode updates. It conditions generation through explicit textual provision or implicit parametric adaptation. To measure evolved-state generation, we introduce LongEvoRoleBench, which pairs four long-dialogue corpora for cross-episode evolution with four short-dialogue corpora as within-scene state-tracking checks, under a unified next-utterance protocol. On the long-dialogue core, textual PHASE-Tree ranks first in 11 of 12 dataset-metric cells against internal variants and all 12 cells against external textual baselines, improving character-level, semantic, and embedding scores by 19.7%, 12.4%, and 15.1% respectively. In a blinded 200-response study, human ratings correlate with the GPT-4.1 judge (Pearson r= 0.65); on descriptive n= 10 PT and NR prompt subsets, the Overall difference is +0.20. The long-dialogue Sem advantage persists across LLM judges and generation backbones.

</details>


### [106] [Confirming Our Biases? Evaluating the Capabilities, Risks, and Societal Impact of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06977)
*Mudar Adas,Polina Tsvilodub,Michael Franke,Martin V. Butz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）对用户提示框架中偏见的敏感程度，通过评估六个LLM在160个提示（涵盖十个主题）上的表现，发现LLM会系统性地调整回答以迎合提示框架，即使在事实性领域也是如此，表明提示框架的影响可以超过事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证实LLMs对提示措辞敏感，但关于LLM在多大程度上会强化用户提示中表达的偏见，以及隐式框架效应与显式提示操纵之间的边界尚不清楚，本文旨在厘清这些问题。

Method: 使用六个LLM，构建160个不同提示，覆盖十个主题，系统变化提示策略（支持/挑战指令）、提示极性、用户表达信念和领域（观点型/事实型），评估模型对直接和暗示性提示的响应一致性与偏差程度。

Result: LLMs会系统性地根据提示框架调整回答，即使在事实性语境下也是如此；提示框架对回答的影响可以超过事实一致性；LLM会强化用户的微妙偏见，并且容易受显式提示操纵影响。

Conclusion: 研究划定了LLM可操纵性的范围和边界，表明LLM在应当保持事实稳定的领域也会受到提示框架的显著影响，这对其安全部署和可靠使用具有重要启示。

Abstract: It is well established that large language models (LLMs) are sensitive to prompt framing, reflecting patterns in their training data or prior prompts. In this study, we investigate the extent to which LLMs reinforce users biases expressed in the prompts and examine the boundary between implicit framing effects and explicit prompt manipulation. Specifically, we evaluate how susceptible LLMs are to direct and suggestive prompts that encourage models to support or challenge particular positions.
  We evaluate six LLMs using 160 distinct prompts spanning ten topics across opinion-based and factual domains. The prompts systematically vary in prompting strategy, support versus challenge instructions, prompt polarity, users' expressed beliefs, and topic domain, spanning both opinion-based and factual questions. Our results show that LLMs systematically adapt their responses to align with prompt framing, even in factual contexts. This suggests that prompt framing can outweigh factual consistency in model responses. Overall, our findings delineate the extent and boundaries of LLM manipulability. Furthermore, the results imply that LLMs can reinforce subtle user biases and are susceptible to explicit prompt manipulation even in domains where responses should remain factually stable.

</details>


### [107] [GPTKB 2.0: Browsing, Querying, and Auditing a Disambiguated LLM-Derived Knowledge Base](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06992)
*Yujia Hu,Tuan-Phong Nguyen,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍GPTKB 2.0，一个从大型语言模型中物化的大规模消歧知识库，包含3840万条三元组、160万规范实体、20.76万关系及6.6万类，与先前按表面字符串识别实体的LLM知识库不同，GPTKB 2.0在递归构建过程中进行上下文引导的消歧，并提供了可视化演示界面供用户浏览、审计和查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前由大型语言模型衍生的知识库大多仅通过表面字符串识别实体，缺乏真正的实体消歧能力，导致同义词无法合并、同形异义词无法区分，限制了知识库的质量与可用性。

Method: GPTKB 2.0在递归知识库构建过程中引入上下文引导的消歧机制，在事实抽取时区分同形异义词并合并同义提及，同时将整个构建过程可视化，支持SPARQL查询、自然语言转SPARQL以及实体链接等功能。

Result: 构建了包含3840万条三元组、160万规范实体、20.76万关系和6.6万类的知识库，提供了可交互的Web演示，用户可通过gptkb.org访问并可下载完整知识库离线使用。

Conclusion: GPTKB 2.0通过上下文引导的消歧显著提升了LLM衍生知识库的实体规范化质量，演示系统使消歧过程透明可审计，为大规模知识库的构建与验证提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a web demo for exploring a large-scale disambiguated knowledge base (KB) materialized from a large language model (LLM). GPTKB 2.0 contains 38.4M triples over 1.6M canonical entities, together with 207.6K consolidated relations and 66K consolidated classes. Unlike prior LLM-derived knowledge bases that largely identify entities by surface strings, GPTKB 2.0 performs context-guided disambiguation during recursive KB construction, separating homonyms and merging synonymous mentions as facts are elicited. The demo makes this process inspectable: users can browse entities, follow links across the KB, and audit the provenance of individual facts, including surface forms, candidate matches, source triples, and disambiguation decisions. The interface further supports structured SPARQL queries, natural-language questions translated to SPARQL, and entity linking from user-provided text to canonical GPTKB 2.0 entries. GPTKB 2.0 is available at https://gptkb.org/, with the full KB downloadable for offline use.

</details>


### [108] [Does More Retrieved Evidence Help Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Diffusion Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07006)
*Jiankun Wang,Yisen Gao,Ziwei Zhang,Xingcheng Fu,Jiaxin Bai,Chen Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ECF（基于熵的候选过滤）框架，针对扩散语言模型（DLM）中的视觉检索增强生成（RAG）问题。研究发现DLM中无条件传递所有检索到的视觉证据反而会降低答案准确率（因语义冲突），因此提出免训练的候选筛选机制，通过多粒度证据单元和blank控制的置信度评估来选择性接纳证据，在三个多模态DLM和五个视觉QA基准上将准确率平均提升2.62个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉RAG假设检索到的所有证据都应传给生成器以覆盖更多答案页面，但该假设在扩散语言模型中不成立——检索更多页面虽提高answer-page召回率，但无条件传入生成器反而因语义冲突降低答案准确率。作者通过潜在源分析发现并行去噪中的源一致性损失是主因，因此需要一种选择性证据接纳机制来平衡检索覆盖率和有害视觉暴露。

Method: 提出Entropy-Based Candidate Filter (ECF)免训练框架。核心包括：(1) 构建多粒度证据单元以减少单个候选内的无关内容；(2) 利用blank控制的块置信度和检索排名判断额外证据是否有益，决定是否以及哪个候选应进入最终上下文；(3) 利用首步答案块分布中已可见的证据干扰信号进行解码前评估。

Result: 在三个多模态DLM和五个视觉QA基准上，ECF相比最强固定top-k输入平均提升2.62个百分点答案准确率；与LLaDA2.0-Uni配合时，相比各数据集最佳免训练基线方法平均提升2.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 更广泛的检索对视觉DLM-RAG的收益需要通过选择性证据接纳而非无条件证据扩展来实现。证据干扰在解码前期即可检测，这为免训练的候选筛选提供了可行性。

Abstract: Visual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) commonly expands the retrieved evidence set to improve answer-page coverage, implicitly assuming that all available evidence should be passed to the generator. We show that this assumption does not hold for diffusion language models (DLMs): retrieving more pages increases answer-page recall, whereas unconditionally passing all retrieved pages to the generator often reduces answer accuracy, primarily because of semantic conflict. A latent-source analysis explains this mismatch through source-coherence loss in parallel denoising, where position-wise proposals can combine incompatible visual sources into unsupported answers. We further find that such interference is already visible in the first-step answer-block distribution, making it possible to assess evidence before decoding. To preserve retrieval coverage while limiting harmful visual exposure, we propose the Entropy-Based Candidate Filter (ECF), a training-free evidence-admission framework. To reduce irrelevant content within individual candidates, ECF constructs multi-granularity evidence units; to identify beneficial additional evidence, it uses blank-controlled block confidence and retrieval rank to determine whether and which candidate should enter the final context. Across three multimodal DLMs and five visual QA benchmarks, ECF improves answer accuracy by 2.62 percentage points on average over the strongest fixed top-$k$ input and, with LLaDA2.0-Uni, by 2.37 percentage points on average over the best competing training-free result for each dataset. These results show that broader retrieval benefits visual DLM-RAG through selective evidence admission rather than unconditional evidence expansion. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/wjkuser/ECF.

</details>


### [109] [An Agentic Hybrid Top-Down and Bottom-Up Approach to Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07023)
*Emma Jouffroy,Warren Jouanneau,Marc Palyart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合知识图谱生成流水线，将大语言模型锚定在Wikidata多语言知识图谱上，结合智能体反思模式合成新兴技能概念及其元数据，在五个阶段中自动适应跨五种欧洲语言的非标准化、嘈杂技能表述，最终生成可扩展、可解释且自愈的技能知识图谱及结构化分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人力资源平台面临数千个非标准化、多语言的技能声明的组织难题，这直接影响人才匹配等下游任务的准确性。

Method: 提出混合知识图谱生成流水线：将LLM锚定在Wikidata多语言知识图谱上，采用智能体反思模式合成新兴概念；系统将已识别概念锚定到稳定的KG实体，同时为未识别技能动态创建新节点和关系元数据；执行五个阶段：实体协调、多语言规范化、主动策展、去重和未映射概念的迭代恢复。

Result: 该系统在五种欧洲语言上自主适应快速演变且嘈杂的技能提及，提供了高度可扩展、可解释且自愈的技能知识图谱生成框架，并从中推导出结构化分类体系。

Conclusion: 该流水线为从未结构化、嘈杂文本中生成全面的技能知识图谱提供了一种高度可扩展、可解释且自愈的框架，并可直接服务于人才匹配等下游任务。

Abstract: Organizing thousands of unstandardized, multilingual expertise declarations is a persistent challenge for Human Resources (HR) platforms, directly impacting downstream tasks like accurate talent matching. To address this, we propose a hybrid knowledge graph generation pipeline that grounds a Large Language Model (LLM) in the Wikidata multilingual Knowledge Graph (KG) while employing an agentic reflexion pattern to synthesize emerging concepts and their associated metadata. Unlike rigid top-down methods or fragmented bottom-up approaches, our system anchors recognized concepts to stable Knowledge Graph entities while dynamically creating new nodes and relational metadata for unrecognized skills. Executed across five stages, entity reconciliation, multilingual canonicalization, active curation, deduplication, and the iterative recovery of unmapped concepts, the system autonomously adapts to rapidly evolving, noisy skill mentions across five European languages. Ultimately, this pipeline provides a highly scalable, explicable, and self-healing framework for generating a comprehensive skills knowledge graph, from which a structured taxonomy is derived, using unstructured, noisy text.

</details>


### [110] [HNR-DAC: Hard-Negative Reranking and Distribution-Aligned Classification for Scientific Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07204)
*Zhenchao Wang,Xin Chen,Luoxi Zhang,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HNR-DAC两阶段框架用于科学论文声明验证任务。第一阶段通过硬负样本重排序（HNR）量化证据混淆度，第二阶段采用分布对齐分类（DAC）在处理与推理阶段一致的数据上训练。在NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2上取得97.21% Hit@3、95.79% Macro-F1、94.47% Joint@3的成绩，官方排行榜位列第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学声明验证面临两个相互关联的挑战：论文内部的干扰段落往往与真实证据高度相似，导致区分困难；同时，在黄金证据上训练的分类器需要在推理时面对检索到的证据，存在训练与推理分布不匹配的问题。

Method: HNR-DAC框架包含两个阶段，每个阶段都在其实际遇到的场景上进行训练。第一阶段（HNR）利用基础重排序器对非黄金段落的评分来量化证据混淆度，将黄金证据与最易混淆的候选进行对比训练。第二阶段（DAC）使用与推理阶段相同的冻结HNR生成的Top-1段落作为训练数据，实现分布对齐；同时输出HNR的Top-3段落标识作为证据结果。

Result: 在NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2上，最终配置获得97.21% Hit@3、95.79% Macro-F1、94.47% Joint@3以及95.13%的平均分。官方提交在Track 2排行榜排名第三，同时取得最高的93.05%总体Macro-F1、70.16% Joint@3和81.61%平均分。

Conclusion: HNR-DAC通过让每个训练阶段匹配其推理时遇到的数据分布，有效解决了论文内干扰段落混淆和训练/推理分布不匹配两个挑战，在科学声明验证任务上取得了优异的性能，验证了分布对齐训练策略的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific claim verification over a cited paper requires predicting the claim--paper relation and identifying the paragraphs that justify that prediction. This setting poses two linked challenges: within-paper distractors often resemble genuine evidence, while a classifier trained on gold evidence must operate on retrieved evidence at inference. We present HNR-DAC, a two-stage framework that trains each stage on the cases it will actually encounter. Hard-Negative Reranking (HNR) quantifies evidence confusability using a base reranker's scores on non-gold paragraphs and contrasts gold evidence against the most confusable candidates. Distribution-Aligned Classification (DAC) trains on the Top-1 paragraph produced by the same frozen HNR used to construct inference inputs, while HNR's Top-3 paragraph identifiers provide the evidence output. On the NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2, the final configuration obtains 97.21% Hit@3, 95.79% Macro-F1, 94.47% Joint@3, and an average score of 95.13%. The corresponding submission ranks third on the official Track 2 leaderboard while achieving the highest overall Macro-F1 of 93.05%, alongside 70.16% Joint@3 and an average score of 81.61%.

</details>


### [111] [Measuring Concept Content in Text from LLM Activations: ESG Evidence from Concept Vectors and Linear Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07208)
*Luc Hazenoot,Zhaochun Ren,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过监测冻结、开箱即用的LLM的内部激活（activations）来测量文本中的概念内容，避免任务特定的微调。在人类标注的ESG金融数据集上，最佳的线性探针（linear probing）准确率仅比微调后的领域分类器低0.6个百分点，且在11/12次比较中优于模型自身回答，表明激活中隐含的概念内容超越了模型文字输出的表层表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本概念测量方法（如词典词频、主题比例、嵌入相似度）只读取文本表面，无法捕捉读者形成的判断。已有研究表明LLM内部知识与外部输出之间存在差距。作者希望探索能否利用这种内部知识（通过监测冻结LLM的激活）来替代任务特定的微调，实现概念内容的测量。

Method: 在冻结、开箱即用的LLM上，通过两种方法提取概念度量：(1) Recursive Feature Machine (RFM)算法提取概念向量；(2) 线性探针（linear probing）。将这两种方法分别与嵌入基线、表面基线（dictionary/topic methods）以及同一模型自身的文字回答进行比较。实验在人类标注的ESG金融数据集上进行。

Result: 最佳线性探针的准确率比微调后的领域分类器仅低0.6个百分点，无需任何任务特定微调；在12次比较中，线性探针在11次上优于同一模型自身的回答；简单的线性探针一致优于RFM概念向量；RFM概念向量提供了分类所不能提供的连续分数，用于反映概念在文本中出现的强度。

Conclusion: LLM的内部激活确实携带了任务相关的概念内容，且这些内容超越了模型在文字输出中表达的信息。线性探针是提取该内部知识最有效的方法，能够在无需微调的情况下接近微调分类器的性能。RFM概念向量提供了连续的强度评分，但其效度仍需带有分级标签的数据集进一步验证。

Abstract: Existing measures of how much a text is about a concept read the surface of the text: dictionary word shares, topic proportions, embedding similarities. They score the words a text uses, not the judgment a reader forms about it. Recent work has shown that a gap exists in what Large Language Models (LLMs) know internally versus what they express in their response. This paper asks whether that internal knowledge, read by monitoring the activations of frozen, out-of-the-box LLMs, can stand in for task-specific fine-tuning when measuring concept content, and which extraction method reads it best. We extract such measures via the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm and via linear probing, and compare these against an embedding baseline, surface baselines, and the same model's own answer to the question. We demonstrate the approach on financial text, a domain studied extensively and served by established annotated resources, using a human-annotated Environmental, Social and Governance (ESG) dataset. The best linear probe comes within 0.6 percentage points of a fine-tuned domain classifier's accuracy without any task-specific fine-tuning, and outscores the same model's own answer to the question in eleven of twelve comparisons, so the activations carry concept content the response does not report. The simple probe consistently beats the RFM concept vectors, which in turn provide what classification alone does not: a continuous score intended to reflect how strongly a concept is present in a text, whose validation awaits graded labels.

</details>


### [112] [From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07213)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yuanxi Che,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究文本到SQL系统中测试时计算（如修复）的未来价值问题，提出"结晶问题"（crystallization problem），通过受控评估分离度量记忆回放（replay）、跨问题保留（cross-question retention）和同库迁移（held-out transfer）的效果。实验表明在BIRD数据集上，存储已验证的修正查询可将未见问题的首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，且数据库特定内容是最主要的有效因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL系统在测试时进行额外计算（如修复）后会将结果丢弃，虽然越来越多的系统会保留已验证的修复片段，但评估仍只报告单一的端到端分数，无法区分记忆回放对重复问题的帮助与对未见问题的帮助，也无法识别起作用的记忆选择。作者将这一度量未来价值的挑战称为"结晶问题"。

Method: 采用受控评估方法：固定单次求解器不变，每次只改变一个记忆选择变量，分别度量三种效果——回放（replay）、跨问题保留（cross-question retention）、同数据库的保留集外迁移（held-out same-database transfer）。在BIRD数据集上进行系统消融实验。

Result: 存储已验证的修正查询使BIRD上未见问题的首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，捕获了同问题上按需修复所提供准确率空间的44.4%。受控干预实验表明：数据库特定内容是主要有效成分；可靠验证和更广泛的检索覆盖能带来有支持的收益，而更丰富的查询格式和更复杂的检索器并无增益。

Conclusion: 记忆（即存储已验证修复）确实能带来超越重复问题的泛化价值，而数据库特定内容是这种价值的关键来源。研究提供开源代码、评估工件和复现说明，为后续系统设计记忆机制提供指导。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can correct difficult text-to-SQL queries, but the extra computation is normally discarded after each answer. Systems increasingly retain verified repair episodes, yet evaluations still report one end-to-end score. It cannot distinguish replay on recurring questions from help on unseen questions, or identify the responsible memory choice. We call measuring this future value the crystallization problem. Our controlled evaluation holds the single-shot solver fixed and varies one memory choice at a time. We separately measure replay, cross-question retention, and held-out same-database transfer. On BIRD, storing verified corrected queries improves held-out first-attempt accuracy by 4.34 percentage points. This gain captures 44.4% of the accuracy headroom provided by on-demand repair on the same questions. Controlled interventions identify database-specific content as the main operating ingredient. Reliable verification and broader retrieval coverage yield supported gains; richer formats and elaborate retrievers do not. Open-source code, evaluation artifacts, and reproduction instructions are available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/text-to-sql-memory-crystallization.

</details>


### [113] [Skaling: Chinchilla's Exponents Meet Kaplan's Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07222)
*Mathurin Videau,Badr Youbi-Idrissi,David Lopez-Paz,Kartik Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Skaling law，一种耦合模型容量与数据量的广义神经扩展律，通过引入单一交互指数修正标准扩展律在数据稀缺和过度训练极端情况下的系统性偏差，MAPE降低1.5-3倍，并配合稀疏网格策略实现约10倍计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的神经扩展律基于模型规模与训练数据对损失的影响相互独立的假设，导致在数据稀缺和过度训练的极端场景下系统性高估或低估损失，无法准确预测语言模型性能。

Method: 提出Skaling law，通过引入单一交互指数来耦合模型容量与数据规模的影响；同时设计稀疏网格策略，仅在低计算量区间采样，实现全网格外推预测。

Result: Skaling law在插值与外推两种场景下将MAPE降低1.5-3倍；结合稀疏网格策略，相比均匀扫描以约10倍更少的计算量实现准确的全网格外推。

Conclusion: Skaling law从小规模实验中实现可靠的性能预测，为下一代模型训练的计算预算分配提供了更稳健、更资源高效的框架。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws are foundational for language model development, yet standard formulations systematically under- and overestimate loss at data-scarce and overtraining extremes. This failure originates in the underlying assumption that model size and training data impact the loss independently. To address this, we introduce the Skaling law, a generalized functional form that couples model capacity and data through a single interaction exponent. This simple extension reduces the Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) by 1.5-3x across both interpolation and extrapolation regimes. When paired with a sparse grid strategy restricted to low-compute regimes, the Skaling law achieves accurate full-grid extrapolation using approximately 10x less compute than uniform sweeps. By enabling reliable performance prediction from small-scale experiments, the Skaling law provides a more robust and resource-efficient framework for allocating compute budgets in next-generation model training.

</details>


### [114] [Stoicheia: Character-Level Masked Diffusion for Ancient Greek Textual Restoration, Parsing, and Metrical Scansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07249)
*Eric Cullhed,Albin Thörn Cleland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Stoicheia，一个405M参数的古希腊语字符级掩码扩散编码器，其输入分解为五个可独立掩码的对齐平面（字母、词/句边界、变音符、大小写、标点），实现单一骨干网络完成文本修复、重新分词、加变音符和标点等多种任务。模型在380M词的开放语料上预训练，发布11个检查点（10个旋转去污染折叠+1个无文献文本暴露）。实验表明字符级扩散预训练在铭文重建（CER提升5.6点）、依存句法分析（LAS提升12.9点）和长音符号化（平衡准确率提升6.0点）上均有显著贡献，且在Ithaca测试集上CER从24.6降至15.5，top-1准确率从63.0提升至74.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语文献（铭文、纸莎草、未分词文本）存在字形残缺、缺乏分隔符等问题，现有模型通常需要针对不同任务进行特定重分词，缺乏统一框架。作者希望构建一个单一模型，能同时处理文本恢复、重新分词、变音符添加和标点恢复等多种任务，同时避免数据污染问题，确保评估的可信度。

Method: 设计405M参数の字符级掩码扩散编码器，输入分解为五个对齐且可独立掩码的平面（字母、词/句边界、变音符、大小写、标点），共用同一骨干网络。在380M词的开放修订固定语料上预训练，发布11个检查点：10个旋转去污染折叠（任意文献片段至少有一个模型从未见过）和1个无文献文本暴露的检查点。每项实验均设置随机初始化对照组以隔离预训练贡献。

Result: 三项实验均显示字符级扩散预训练带来显著提升：铭文重建CER改善5.6个点，依存句法分析LAS提升12.9个点，长音符号化平衡准确率提升6.0个点。在Ithaca测试集上（相同冻结样本、严格评分），CER从Ithaca的24.6和2025 Aeneas框架后继版的23.5降至15.5，top-1准确率从63.0和64.0提升至74.5。

Conclusion: Stoicheia证明字符级掩码扩散预训练是处理古希腊语多种下游任务的统一有效方案，通过多平面可掩码分解和去污染数据发布策略，在文本恢复、句法分析和语音标注等任务上均显著超越现有最佳系统。

Abstract: We introduce Stoicheia, a 405M-parameter character-level masked-diffusion encoder for Ancient Greek whose input factors into five aligned, independently maskable planes: letters, word and sentence boundaries, diacritics, capitalization, and punctuation. A single backbone can therefore restore lacunae, re-segment, accentuate, and punctuate unspaced text without task-specific retokenization. We pretrain it on an open, revision-pinned corpus of 380M words and release eleven checkpoints: ten rotated, decontaminated folds, guaranteeing that for any given literary passage at least one released model has never seen its text, and one with no exposure to documentary texts. Three experiments - reconstruction of damaged inscriptions and papyri, morphosyntactic tagging and dependency parsing, and macronization with metrical scansion - each carry a matched random-initialization control, isolating what character-level diffusion pretraining contributes: 5.6 CER points on inscription reconstruction, 12.9 LAS on parsing, and 6.0 points of balanced accuracy on macronization. On Ithaca's own test split, with identical frozen samples and strict scoring, Stoicheia reduces character error relative to both prior state-of-the-art systems, from 24.6 (Ithaca) and 23.5 (its 2025 Aeneas-framework successor) to 15.5, and raises top-1 accuracy from 63.0 and 64.0 to 74.5.

</details>


### [115] [Why Knowing Both Hops Is Not Enough: Understanding Two-Hop Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07261)
*Zili Zhang,Yilin Wang,Heng Wang,Herun Wan,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过可控制的符号环境从头训练Transformer，揭示了LLM在简单两跳查询上泛化失败的根本机制：当第二跳偏离训练分布时，底层正确构建中间表示，但顶层仅学会将表示映射到输出而非推理，并提出了循环式训练策略来改善跨输入形式的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能解决复杂多跳问题，却在简单的两跳查询上表现出令人困惑的失败：模型可能正确存储每个单跳信息，却无法将它们组合起来。理解这一内部机制是解决该问题的关键。

Method: 在可控符号环境中从头训练Transformer，通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis）深入研究两跳泛化行为，并基于发现提出循环式（recurrent-style）训练策略。

Result: 发现两跳泛化呈现清晰规律：当第二跳符合训练分布时模型可靠泛化，偏离时则总是失败。成功的泛化依赖于同一实体跨上下文一致中间表示的出现；失败源于层间不匹配——底层正确构建中间表示，而顶层仅学习将表示映射到输出而非据此推理。提出的循环式训练策略使Transformer重用推理电路，显著改善分布外两跳查询的泛化。

Conclusion: 两跳推理失败的根本原因在于模型中不同层承担的角色不对称：底层负责构建表示，顶层默认将其当作直接映射的输入而非推理对象。通过循环式训练强制实现推理电路复用，可以有效提升分布外两跳泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve complex multi-hop problems yet exhibit puzzling failures on simple two-hop queries: although a model may correctly store each individual hop, it often fails to combine them. To understand the internal mechanisms of this phenomenon, we train transformers from scratch in a controlled symbolic environment. Our experiments reveal a pattern in two-hop generalization: models generalize reliably when the second hop follows the training distribution, but always fail when it deviates.
  Through mechanistic analysis, we provide a complete explanation for these distinct generalization behaviors: in settings where models generalize successfully, performance is driven by the emergence of consistent intermediate representations for the same entities across contexts, whereas failures on settings where the second hop is out-of-distribution arise from a mismatch across layers: lower layers correctly construct these intermediate representations, but upper layers, while trained on corresponding atomic facts, primarily learn to map them to outputs rather than to reason over them.
  Driven by this insight, we propose a recurrent-style training strategy, which enables transformers to reuse their reasoning circuitry across input forms and substantially improves generalization on out-of-distribution two-hop queries.

</details>


### [116] [Gaze Behavior in Visual World Experiments Can be Modeled With Off-the-shelf Language-Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07282)
*Rahul Murali Shankar,Titus von der Malsburg,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测视觉世界研究中人类注视行为的新方法，通过结合CLIP家族的多模态双编码器模型与双模态归因方法，在不进行微调且无需生成架构的情况下，成功复现了一项经典的英语视觉世界研究结果，证明了该方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经语言模型在计算心理语言学中已被用于研究人类语言处理，但绝大多数工作仅聚焦于单模态（书面或口语）场景，忽略了如视觉世界研究这类同时呈现视觉与语言输入的多模态实验范式。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用CLIP家族的多模态双编码器模型，结合一种双模态归因方法，用以预测视觉世界研究中人类受试者的注视行为。

Result: 该方法能够稳健地复现一项具有开创性的英语视觉世界研究结果，该研究展示了人类语言的预测性加工能力。值得注意的是，该方法无需生成式架构、无需微调，且模型并非为这一任务专门训练。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，简单的判别式多模态模型结合归因方法即可有效模拟人类在多模态语言理解中的预测性注视行为，为多模态心理语言学计算建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: The recent advances in neural language models have also spurred much work in computational psycholinguistics, asking whether neural LMs are also promising models of human language processing. However, work has been overwhelmingly focused on the unimodal case of written or spoken language. In contrast, multimodal experimental paradigms, like visual world studies that present participants with both visual and linguistic input simultaneously, have been neglected. In this paper, we present a novel approach that predicts gaze behavior in visual world studies. It does so by combining a simple multi-modal bi-encoder model of the CLIP family with a bimodal attribution method. We demonstrate the ability of this approach to robustly replicate the results of a seminal English visual world study which shows hu- man predictive processing. Remarkably, it does so without a generative architecture and without the need for fine-tuning, despite not being trained for this task.

</details>


### [117] [Grammar Engineering Meets LLMs: Development of Cantonese and Irish ParGram Treebanks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07283)
*Chit-Fung Lam,Elaine Uí Dhonnchadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在ParGram平行语法项目中开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库的过程，并探索了使用多语言大语言模型（LLM）辅助语法工程的潜力与局限。研究发现LLM在翻译任务上表现不佳且不受提示语言影响，在句法结构生成上虽能产生部分有意义的输出但跨语言抽象能力较弱，最终强调专家驱动分析与验证依然不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法工程需要同时具备语言形式化专业知识和计算实现能力，尤其在平行语法项目中需要在跨语言一致性与语言特性之间取得平衡。本文旨在探索LLM能否作为辅助工具支持这一复杂过程，并评估其方法论上的潜力与局限。

Method: 在ParGram项目框架下开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库，在抽象功能层面维持语言间的平行性；同时使用OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b模型进行粤语-爱尔兰语翻译实验和形式句法结构生成实验，考察提示语言对翻译效果的影响以及模型在跨语言句法抽象任务上的表现。

Result: 翻译性能总体不理想且不受提示语言影响；句法结构生成方面，模型能产出部分结构上有意义的输出，但在需要跨语言抽象的任务上表现较差。尽管如此，LLM生成的输出仍可提供参考价值，如建议替代分析方案和部分捕获谓词-论元关系。

Conclusion: LLM在协作式语法工程中既有潜力也有明显局限，专家驱动的分析与验证仍然是保证质量的关键。

Abstract: Grammar engineering requires expertise in linguistic formalism and computational implementation, especially in parallel grammar projects that balance cross-linguistic consistency with language-specific properties. This paper presents the development of Cantonese and Irish treebanks within the Parallel Grammar (ParGram) Project, where linguistic parallelism is maintained at an abstract functional level. We also investigate the methodological potential and limitations of using multilingual LLMs to support grammar engineering, focusing on Cantonese-Irish translation and the generation of formal syntactic structures using OpenAI's gpt-oss-120b model. The results show that translation performance was generally unsatisfactory and unaffected by prompt language. For syntactic structure generation, the model produced some structurally meaningful outputs, but performed poorly on tasks requiring cross-linguistic abstraction. Nonetheless, LLM-generated outputs may still offer some reference value by suggesting alternative analyses and (partially) capturing predicate-argument relations. Overall, our findings highlight both the potential and limitations of using LLMs in collaborative grammar engineering, while underscoring the continued importance of expert-driven analysis and verification.

</details>


### [118] [Natural Language Processing Psychometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07316)
*Edoardo Sebastiano De Duro,Emma Franchino,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NLP心理测量学框架，利用九个LLM人格模拟（认知数字影子）配合问卷回答，提取情感特征与语法语义网络结构，结合人口统计学变量构建随机森林回归模型，在生活满意度、抑郁、焦虑、压力等心理指标上解释了高达76%的方差，并能在无需重新训练的情况下对真实人类文本进行心理预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康预测模型不明确其测量内容（是上下文知识、情感内容还是句法结构），缺乏可解释性和可验证性，本文旨在将心理文本预测视为心理测量学问题，使测量内容明确、可测量、可检验。

Method: 九个LLM在受控人格（认知数字影子）条件下完成心理问卷并给出逐项文字解释，从中提取情感画像和文本forma mentis网络（语法-语义结构），结合人格和人口统计学变量输入消融随机森林回归模型，使用SHAP分析特征贡献方向。

Result: 全模型可解释生活满意度（SWLS）70.8%、抑郁（PHQ-9）55.7%的方差；DASS-21上抑郁68.5%、焦虑76.0%、压力72.4%。仅人口统计学变量对抑郁焦虑压力无显著解释力，但对生活满意度有效；神经质和网络拓扑主导抑郁和焦虑预测且方向相反。零训练状态下RF模型可区分日记与高低分人格（r最高0.91），仅用网络/情感特征对真实转录的临床-对照分类准确率达68%。

Conclusion: LLM人格模拟能暴露模型偏见、恢复与临床反刍一致的模式，并可支持无需匹配问卷的人类文本心理测量预测，但无法替代人类验证；NLP心理测量学通过可解释AI和网络/情感特征使测量区分明确化、可量化、可检验。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) models predicting mental health outcomes rarely specify what they measure: contextual knowledge, emotional content, or syntactic structure. NLP Psychometrics treats psychological prediction from text as a psychometric problem, linking scores to interpretable linguistic evidence and testing beyond the training text format. Nine LLMs, conditioned on controlled personas (cognitive digital shadows), completed psychometric questionnaires with textual explanations per item. We extracted emotional profiles and syntactic-semantic structure via textual forma mentis networks, combined with personality and sociodemographic variables in ablated random forest (RF) regressors, using SHAP to identify which features drove performance and in which direction. Full RF models explained up to 70.8% of variance in life satisfaction (SWLS), 55.7% in depression (PHQ-9), and, for DASS-21, 68.5% depression, 76.0% anxiety, 72.4% stress. Sociodemographics alone explained no meaningful variance in depression, anxiety, or stress, but did so for life satisfaction, where emotion features and income were the strongest predictors; neuroticism and network topology instead dominated depression and anxiety, reversing direction between them. Without retraining, RF models separated diaries from low- and high-score personas ($r$ up to 0.91) and, using only network/emotion features, classified clinical from control participants in real transcripts with up to 68% accuracy. These results show the promise and limits of synthetic data: LLM personas can expose model biases, recover patterns consistent with clinical rumination, and support psychometric prediction from human text without a matched questionnaire, but cannot substitute for human validation. NLP Psychometrics makes these distinctions explicit, measurable, and testable through interpretable AI and network/emotional features.

</details>


### [119] [Zero Gap Is Not Restoration: Stratified Per-Question Probability Evaluation and Step-wise Mitigation of Benchmark Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07341)
*Ruijie Hou,Yueyang Jiao,Zhao Wang,Yingming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有污染缓解评估指标G-AP存在缺陷，提出新指标SA-PPG以更准确评估污染缓解效果，并设计了一种无需预先估计污染位置的新方法RailCap，在实验中取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准测试数据不可避免地泄漏到预训练语料中，导致模型记忆后评估分数虚高。现有污染缓解评估指标G-AP存在三个关键缺陷：离散的正确/错误读数无法刻画每道题的连续表现；先平均后作差使过度抑制与抑制不足相互抵消；均匀的逐题权重容易诱导策略将解概率推到干净模型的高频值上。

Method: 作者提出SA-PPG指标：通过采样估计每道题的求解概率，与干净模型逐题作差，再按干净模型求解概率分组聚合。同时提出RailCap方法：在生成过程中实时判断污染，当采样回落到贪婪轨迹时，将下一轨迹token限制为次优选项，逐步累积抑制直到响应分布充分分散。

Result: 在多个受污染模型和基准上的实验表明，SA-PPG揭示出先前策略的能力恢复效果被显著高估，而RailCap取得了最低的SA-PPG值。

Conclusion: G-AP作为污染缓解评估指标存在根本性缺陷，SA-PPG能更准确地度量缓解效果；同时传统的"先估计污染再操作"的缓解策略受限于估计准确性，RailCap通过在生成中直接判断污染，避免了这一瓶颈，实现了更好的污染抑制效果。

Abstract: Test data from public benchmarks inevitably leaks into pretraining corpora, inflating evaluation scores once memorized. \textbf{Contamination mitigation evaluation} intervenes in the decoding process to suppress memorization and restore a contaminated model's genuine capability, but its prevailing metric, the \textbf{G-AP} (\textbf{G}ap of \textbf{A}ggregate \textbf{P}erformance), is flawed. Discrete correct/incorrect readouts cannot characterize per-question performance, averaging before differencing lets over- and under-suppression cancel out, and uniform per-question weighting invites strategies to push solve probabilities onto the clean model's high-frequency values. We propose \textbf{SA-PPG} (\textbf{S}tratified \textbf{A}ggregate of \textbf{P}er-question \textbf{P}robability \textbf{G}aps): estimate each question's solve probability by sampling, difference it against the clean model per question, and aggregate within groups defined by the clean model's solve probability. Existing mitigation strategies first estimate where contamination lies and then operate on the estimate, so they are only as correct as the estimate. \textbf{RailCap} instead judges contamination during generation: whenever a sample falls back onto the greedy trajectory, the next trajectory token is capped to the runner-up, accumulating suppression until the response distribution becomes sufficiently dispersed. Across multiple contaminated models and benchmarks, SA-PPG reveals that prior strategies' restoration is substantially overestimated, while RailCap attains the lowest SA-PPG.

</details>


### [120] [Geo-Spatial Concept Probing of Large Language Models: Abstraction, Compositionality, and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07353)
*Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文设计了一个以空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）为核心的基准测试，通过提问回答任务系统性探测LLM在概念抽象、组合性和接地性三个核心属性上的理解能力，并跨多种模型架构和训练范式进行实验，发现当前LLM在概念理解上存在明显局限，并提出了改进概念型LLM设计的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM概念理解的评估要么使用自然语言基准（容易混淆多种技能），要么使用范围狭窄的合成任务（缺乏对底层概念及其属性的精确控制），难以准确、可控地探测LLM的真实概念理解能力。

Method: 围绕概念的核心属性（抽象性、组合性、接地性）设计测试，构建以空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）为中心的概念基准，使用问答任务作为概念理解的代理，在多种LLM架构和训练范式上进行大规模实验，分析模型规模和设计对概念理解的影响。

Result: 实验揭示了当前LLM在概念理解上存在明显局限，并识别出影响模型获取和组合结构化概念能力的关键因素（如模型规模和设计选择）。

Conclusion: 研究为如何重新设计概念型LLM以改善信息访问和知识管理提供了洞见，并公开了代码以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Understanding concepts is fundamental to generalization. Despite their impressive performance on a wide range of tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with genuine concept understanding. Prior work has evaluated conceptual understanding in LLMs using natural-language benchmarks or narrowly scoped synthetic tasks, but these settings often conflate multiple skills or lack precise control over the underlying concepts and their properties. To support controlled probing of concepts in LLMs, we design tests on their core properties: abstraction, compositionality, and groundness. We set up a concept-centric benchmark, targeting spatial concepts such as direction, distance, topology, and their compositions, and use question answering tasks serving as a proxy. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple LLM architectures and training regimes to analyze how model scale and design impact conceptual understanding. The results reveal clear limitations in current LLMs and provide insights into the factors shaping their ability to acquire and compose structured concepts. Our findings shed light on how concept-based LLMs can be redesigned for improved information access and knowledge management. The code will be available at https://github.com/rd20karim/concept-probing.

</details>


### [121] [LitTraceQA: A Benchmark for Multi-Stage Grounding and Verification in Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07370)
*Xuye Liu,Yimu Wang,Peng Shi,Bo Xue,Xiangrui Ke,Songcheng Cai,Kath Choi,Di Wu,Freda Shi,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LitTraceQA是一个面向科学文献的问答基准，要求系统在给定研究问题和论文元数据池的情况下，返回论文标识、证据定位和多种格式的答案，并分别评估论文检索、证据溯源和答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献被广泛用作语言模型和检索增强生成系统的知识来源，但回答研究问题不能仅依赖流畅生成，系统必须能够定位相关论文、找到具体证据并生成忠实于证据的答案，目前缺乏一个同时评估这些能力的基准。

Method: 构建LitTraceQA基准，包含三类关联输出（论文标识、证据位置、答案格式），覆盖表格、图表、文本段落、公式/算法和引文上下文五种证据类型；开发集含55个示例（26个隐藏源单论文问题和29个多论文问题），并提供金标准论文、证据注释和答案用于本地验证；此外构建了包含4,978条唯一问题记录、对应4,859篇唯一金标准论文的大规模最终注释集。

Result: 公开开发集包含55个示例及完整金标准注释，最终注释集包含4,978条唯一问题记录和4,859篇唯一金标准论文。

Conclusion: 通过将论文检索、证据溯源和答案准确性分离评估，LitTraceQA为科学问答系统提供了测试平台，推动系统生成可验证答案而非无依据摘要。

Abstract: Scientific literature is increasingly used as a knowledge source for language models, retrieval-augmented generation systems, and research assistants, but answering research questions from papers requires more than fluent generation. A reliable system must identify the relevant papers, locate the concrete evidence that supports the answer, and produce a response that is faithful to that evidence. We present LitTraceQA, a benchmark for literature-grounded question answering over scientific papers. Given a research question and a metadata pool of papers, a system must return three connected outputs: canonical paper identifiers, supporting evidence locations, and answers in one or more requested formats, including free-form text, multiple-choice answers, and structured tables. LitTraceQA targets evidence types common in scientific reading: tables, figures, text spans, equations or algorithms, and citation contexts. The public development split contains 55 examples, including 26 hidden-source single-paper questions and 29 multi-paper questions, and provides gold papers, evidence annotations, and answers for local validation. We also analyze a larger final annotation collection with 4,978 unique-question records over 4,859 unique gold papers. By evaluating paper retrieval, evidence grounding, and answer accuracy separately, LitTraceQA provides a testbed for scientific QA systems that produce verifiable answers rather than unsupported summaries.

</details>


### [122] [An Exploratory Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Rewriting of Moderate-Complexity Financial Sentences for DisCoCat-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07439)
*Brian Llinas,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子自然语言处理（QNLP）框架下，利用大语言模型辅助的受控重写技术，将中等复杂度的金融情感分析句子预处理为解析器兼容、电路高效的变体，相比仅限低复杂度句子的基线方法，压缩变体在电路层面减少了超过70%的量子比特和门数量，GPT-4.1-mini配合特定提示词达到最高平均准确率0.550±0.035。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的金融情感分析工作发现DisCoCat框架存在实际局限性，包括解析器敏感、模拟成本高以及难以处理较长句子。作者希望探索一种预处理工作流，使中等复杂度句子也能在该量子框架下使用。

Method: 采用LLM辅助的受控重写工作流，对中等复杂度的金融情感句子进行压缩、简化或分解，使其成为解析器兼容且电路高效的变体，同时保留情感相关含义。比较了不同的提示策略、语言模型和过滤配置，并以Stein等人的低复杂度DisCoCat基线作为对照。

Result: 最强的压缩变体在电路层面使平均量子比特和门数量相对原始中等复杂度子集减少超过70%。GPT-4.1-mini配合Prompt B在多次训练中获得最高平均准确率0.550±0.035，优于基线的0.521±0.050。训练集规模与准确率呈中度负相关（Pearson r=-0.446）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助重写为中等复杂度输入在评估的DisCoCat配置中的可用性提供了探索性证据，同时提示了提示词设计、过滤和电路感知预处理是更可扩展的QNLP金融情感分析的重要考虑因素。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) provides a grammar-aware framework for text modeling, and Distributional Compositional Categorical (DisCoCat) is one of its theoretically grounded formulations. Prior work on financial sentiment analysis has identified practical limitations of DisCoCat, including parser sensitivity, high simulation cost, and difficulty handling longer sentences. We study an LLM-assisted preprocessing workflow that uses controlled rewriting to compress, simplify, or decompose moderate-complexity financial sentiment sentences into parser-compatible, circuit-efficient variants while preserving sentiment-bearing meaning. We compare prompting strategies, language models, and filtering configurations with the low-complexity-only DisCoCat baseline of Stein et al. At the circuit level, the strongest compression variants reduce average qubit and gate counts by more than 70 percent relative to the raw moderate-complexity subset. Across repeated training runs, GPT-4.1-mini with Prompt B achieves the highest observed mean accuracy, $0.550 \pm 0.035$, compared with $0.521 \pm 0.050$ for the baseline. Larger training splits do not necessarily improve downstream performance; across evaluated configurations, training-split size has a moderately negative association with accuracy (Pearson $r=-0.446$). These results provide exploratory evidence that LLM-assisted rewriting can make some moderate-complexity inputs usable within the evaluated DisCoCat configuration, while highlighting prompt design, filtering, and circuit-aware preprocessing as considerations for more scalable QNLP-based financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [123] [CoinRAG: Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07458)
*Gyuwan Kim,Cheoneum Park,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoinRAG提出了一种细粒度的KV缓存复用方法，通过将检索块分解为语义单元（nuggets）并选择性组装相关片段的KV表示，在低预填充延迟约束下同时提升RAG的效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG优化工作虽然通过块级KV缓存复用来避免重复处理长检索上下文，但粗粒度的块仍包含大量信息冗余和噪声，在低延迟约束下难以同时满足效率和准确性的Pareto最优。

Method: CoinRAG采用两阶段检索来识别检索块中与查询相关的语义单元（nuggets），然后将这些细粒度单元预计算的KV缓存切片与块级上下文组合，形成紧凑且语义相关的上下文表示。

Result: 在LongBench多跳问答任务上的大量评估表明，CoinRAG显著降低了运营成本，在标准快速预填充延迟预算内，相较其他基线实现了新的Pareto前沿，答案质量（F1）平均相对提升5.3%。

Conclusion: CoinRAG验证了从粗粒度块级到细粒度语义单元级的KV缓存复用是可行的优化方向，能够在效率和准确性之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Recent optimization studies on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have exploited chunk-level KV cache reuse to avoid processing long retrieved contexts for higher efficiency, while significant information redundancy and noise still remain in the coarse-grained chunks. This paper optimizes the Pareto frontier under low prefill latency constraints while maximizing accuracy by proposing CoinRAG (Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG). The name metaphorically reflects our core mechanism: much like assembling small tokens (or "coins") to accumulate a larger value, CoinRAG compositionally reuses offline-computed, fine-grained nugget caches to form a learned contextual representation efficiently in a more semantically relevant but compact manner. Specifically, instead of full-chunk encoding, CoinRAG identifies query-relevant semantic units within retrieved chunks through two-stage retrieval and seamlessly assembles their sliced KV representations with a chunk-level context. Extensive evaluations on LongBench multi-hop question answering tasks demonstrate that CoinRAG significantly reduces operational costs and outperforms the other baselines with a new Pareto frontier and an average 5.3% relative improvement in answer quality (F1) under a standard fast prefill latency budget.

</details>


### [124] [CreativeInstruct: Scalably Teaching LLMs to Balance Quality, Creativity, and Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07460)
*Ananya Sahu,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CreativeInstruct，一种可扩展的指令微调方法，通过引入特殊的[StartCreativity]标记来教导LLM在保持后训练模型质量的同时平衡创造性生成，并引入基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标，在叙事生成和强化学习（RL）任务中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽然提升了LLM的能力，但降低了其输出多样性和创造力，这对需要创造力的任务（如故事生成和强化学习）产生负面影响。

Method: 1) 提出CreativeInstruct指令微调方法，通过学习注入特殊的[StartCreativity]标记来引导生成偏向创造性；2) 提出基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标，捕捉词汇和语义指标遗漏的叙事层面变化。

Result: 在叙事生成中，CreativeInstruct在多样性和质量上匹配或超越多模型基线及蒸馏变体；人工评估中70.3%的案例认为其比后训练LLM更具创造性；在RL任务中，GRPO应用于CreativeInstruct检查点在AMC上提升约4%、在MATH上提升约5个百分点。

Conclusion: CreativeInstruct能够在不牺牲质量且无需推理时使用多个模型的情况下，实现创造性与质量的平衡，并可作为强化学习底座的更优选择。

Abstract: While post-training improves the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), it generally lowers their output diversity and creativity, negatively impacting tasks that explicitly require creativity (e.g., story generation) as well as those that require it implicitly, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL). We instead propose CreativeInstruct, a scalable instruction-tuning method that teaches LLMs to balance creative, base-model-like generations with the quality of post-trained models, by learning to inject special [StartCreativity] spans that bias generation toward creativity. Furthermore, we introduce a structural diversity metric based on graph edit distance, which captures narrative level variation missed by purely lexical and semantic metrics. On narrative generation, CreativeInstruct matches or exceeds the diversity of both multi-model baselines and distilled variants of their outputs, without sacrificing quality or requiring multiple models at inference time. These results are mirrored in our human evaluation, where we find that annotators rate CreativeInstruct generations as more creative than the post-trained LLMs' generations in 70.3% of cases. We also show the benefits of creative models as a substrate for RL: GRPO applied to a CreativeInstruct checkpoint improves by ~4% on AMC and ~5% points on MATH over the same training applied to the post-trained checkpoint.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Beyond Visibility: Real-Time Surface Accessibility Fields from Sparse LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06412)
*Bradley Scott,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Accessibility Field：一种从流式稀疏LiDAR数据中实时估计每个表面点对特定工具可达性的方法，完全在GPU上运行，无需预先场景模型或固定基座，在移动平台上以传感器频率更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D感知系统仅停留在几何重建或可见性估计层面，无法回答"特定工具能否物理接触某个表面"这一机器人交互的基本问题。几何可达性方法需要完整无噪声的网格和固定的运动学基座，无法适用于增量建图的移动平台；可见性估计无法考虑工具几何形状和接近走廊的间隙约束。

Method: 该方法为每个表面点分配一个Accessibility Field标签，通过GPU上预计算的几何核（代表工具在多个旋转接近方向上的形状）评估工具碰撞和接近走廊间隙。采用扫描中心截断有符号距离场（TSDF）集成方案，仅更新每个观测返回附近的体素，而非逐帧投影所有视锥体素，这对非重复扫描传感器（如Livox Mid-360）至关重要。

Result: 在合成物体和成熟Pinus radiata模型上评估：混合可达性几何上F1=90.8（对比Hidden Point Removal基线69.8）；正确识别56.8%的松树枝表面为不可达（尽管从传感器可见）。系统可在工作站和Jetson Orin边缘硬件上实时运行。

Conclusion: 这是首个无需先验场景模型或固定基座框架、从流式稀疏LiDAR实时估计逐点表面可达性的方法，提供了可见性估计无法实现的能力。

Abstract: Understanding which surfaces in a scene are physically accessible to a given tool is fundamental for robotic interaction, yet 3D perception systems typically stop at geometric reconstruction or visibility estimation. Existing geometric accessibility methods require complete, noise-free meshes and fixed kinematic bases, assumptions that fail for mobile platforms mapping incrementally from live data; visibility estimation cannot account for tool geometry or approach-corridor clearance. We propose the Accessibility Field: a per-point labelling of surface accessibility for a given tool, produced in real time from streaming sparse LiDAR and updated at sensor rate as the platform moves. Running entirely on GPU, our method evaluates each surface point against precomputed geometry kernels representing the tool at a set of rotated approach orientations, checking tool collisions and approach-corridor clearance. A scan-centric Truncated Signed Distance Field integration scheme underpins our system, updating only voxels near each observed return rather than projecting every frustum voxel each frame -- critical for nonrepetitive sensors like the Livox Mid-360, where some bins contain no returns. Our system is tool-agnostic, needs no prior scene model, and runs on workstation and Jetson Orin edge hardware. We evaluate quantitatively on synthetic objects and mature-scale Pinus radiata models, showing visibility alone is insufficient as an accessibility proxy: our method achieves F1=90.8 vs. 69.8 for a Hidden Point Removal baseline on mixed-accessibility geometry, and correctly identifies 56.8% of pine branch surfaces as inaccessible despite being visible from the sensor. To our knowledge, this is the first method to estimate per-point surface accessibility in real time from streaming sparse LiDAR without a prior scene model or fixed base frame -- a capability visibility estimation cannot provide.

</details>


### [126] [Implicit Neural Speckle Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06574)
*Matthew R. Ziemann,Casey J. Pellizzari,Tyler J. Hardy,Christopher A. Metzler*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练框架，结合时空隐式神经表示与孔径感知的最大似然公式，直接从含噪观测中恢复动态无散斑图像，无需重新训练即可适应任意瞳面几何，并通过FFT加速算子、随机近似和共轭梯度实现高效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散斑噪声作为乘性、空间相关的相干噪声，严重阻碍相干成像的清晰度。动态场景中无法利用传统的散斑平均方法，去噪尤为困难，现有方法难以在无需标注数据的情况下平衡空间保真度与时间一致性。

Method: 采用时空隐式神经表示（INR）参数化去散斑图像，结合孔径感知的最大似然框架显式建模孔径依赖的散斑空间协方差；通过矩阵自由实现（FFT加速算子、随机近似、共轭梯度）保证计算可行性；引入盲留出准则实现无需干净参考的自动早停。

Result: 在仿真与实验室数据集上，与经典无监督及监督基线相比，该方法在空间保真度、时间一致性和对不同散斑统计特征的鲁棒性方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 该免训练框架为动态相干成像中的散斑去除提供了有效且通用的解决方案，能够适配任意瞳面几何并摆脱对干净参考数据的依赖。

Abstract: Speckle fundamentally limits coherent imaging by introducing multiplicative, spatially correlated noise that obscures scene structure. Removing speckle noise from dynamic scenes--that do not benefit from conventional speckle averaging--is particularly challenging. We introduce a training-free framework that combines a spatiotemporal implicit neural representation with an aperture-aware maximum-likelihood formulation to recover dynamic, speckle-free imagery directly from noisy observations. The coherent likelihood explicitly models the aperture-dependent spatial covariance of speckle, enabling adaptation to arbitrary pupil geometries without retraining. A matrix-free implementation based on FFT-accelerated operators, stochastic approximations, and conjugate gradients makes optimization practical for realistic image sizes. Meanwhile, a blind holdout criterion provides automatic early stopping without clean reference data. Simulated and laboratory results demonstrate improved spatial fidelity, temporal consistency, and robustness to varying speckle statistics relative to classical, unsupervised, and supervised baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Spatial Masked-Set Learning for Sparse Multi-Shell Diffusion MRI Signal Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06958)
*Yousef Sadegheih,Pratibha Kumari,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于空间遮蔽集合（spatial masked-set）的框架，用于稀疏多壳扩散MRI信号合成。该模型将观测信号视为无序集合，利用局部3×3×3邻域空间上下文预测中心体素的径向6阶SHORE系数，并通过解析解码在任意q-space位置合成信号。在HCP100白质体素上，该方法在b=1000、10个梯度输入下达到2.70%的NMSE，相较最先进的连续模型降低22.4%的相对误差。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集多壳扩散MRI能提供丰富的q空间信息，但采集时间长，临床上难以承受。如何在稀疏采样的条件下高效、准确地合成密集信号，是扩散MRI成像加速的核心挑战。

Method: 提出空间遮蔽集合框架：1) 将观测测量视为无序集合输入；2) 使用局部3×3×3体素邻域获取空间上下文；3) 预测中心体素的径向6阶SHORE系数；4) 通过解析解码在任意q空间位置合成信号。训练策略包括壳梯度丢弃、密集信号监督和旋转一致的SHORE目标，确保稀疏输入在增强下与系数监督对齐。

Result: 在HCP100白质体素上评估，保留参考采集的有限扩散加权信号子集。该方法在信号NMSE上优于解析q空间模型和最先进的连续dMRI信号合成模型。b=1000且10个输入梯度时达到2.70% NMSE，相对降低22.4%。在FA指标上解析模型仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架有效结合了无序集合建模与SHORE系数预测的优势，在稀疏多壳扩散MRI信号合成任务上显著优于现有方法，同时提供任意q空间位置合成的灵活性。FA分析表明信号NMSE与张量衍生指标并非完全对应，未来可进一步优化下游指标对齐性。

Abstract: Dense multi-shell diffusion MRI provides rich q-space information but requires long acquisition times. We propose a spatial masked-set framework for sparse multi-shell diffusion MRI signal synthesis. The model treats observed measurements as an unordered set, uses a local $3 \times 3 \times 3$ neighborhood for spatial context, and predicts radial-order-6 SHORE coefficients for the center voxel. The coefficients can then be decoded analytically to synthesize signals at arbitrary q-space locations. Training combines shell-wise gradient dropping, dense signal supervision, and rotation-consistent SHORE targets so that sparse input signals remain aligned with their coefficient supervision under augmentation. We evaluate on held-out HCP100 white-matter voxels by retaining limited subsets of measured diffusion-weighted signals from the reference acquisition. The proposed method achieves lower signal NMSE than both analytical q-space models and a state-of-the-art continuous dMRI signal synthesis model designed for arbitrary input and output q-space sampling. In the $b=1000$ setting with 10 input gradients, it achieves $2.70\%$ NMSE, a $22.4\%$ relative reduction over this continuous model. Fractional anisotropy on reconstructed $b=1000$ signals provides a complementary tensor-derived endpoint, with analytical models remaining competitive for FA despite higher dense-signal NMSE across the evaluated q-space. The implementation is available on \href{https://github.com/xmindflow/SHOREPred}{https://github.com/xmindflow/SHOREPred}.

</details>


### [128] [IceHorizon: A Dataset for Horizon Detection in Ice-Covered Maritime Environments and Comparative Evaluation of Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07018)
*Alisa Pesotskaia,Emin Zerman*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文对六种地平线检测算法（四种经典计算机视觉方法和两种深度学习与经典线检测混合方法）进行了比较评估，使用新构建的IceHorizon数据集（30个船载视频和8个无人机视频）。结果显示混合方法准确率最高、可靠性最好，而纯经典方法在视觉模糊场景中鲁棒性较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰覆盖水域图像中的地平线检测对海上航行具有挑战性，因为水域与天空对比度低、冰结构杂乱且光照条件多变，需要系统比较现有算法的性能。

Method: 构建了一个包含38个视频（30个船载+8个无人机）的IceHorizon数据集，对六种算法（四种经典计算机视觉方法+两种混合深度学习方法）进行检测精度、地平线覆盖率和计算性能三个维度的对比评估。

Result: 混合方法在准确性和可靠性方面表现最佳；纯经典方法在视觉模糊场景中鲁棒性下降；船载图像性能普遍高于无人机图像，表明检测效果高度依赖采集条件。

Conclusion: 混合深度学习方法更适合冰覆盖水域的地平线检测任务，数据集和代码已公开以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Horizon detection in images of ice-covered waters is a challenging problem for maritime navigation due to low contrast between water and sky, cluttered ice structures, and varying illumination conditions. This paper presents a comparative evaluation of six horizon detection algorithms, including four classical computer vision methods and two hybrid approaches combining deep learning with classical line detection. A new bespoke IceHorizon dataset consisting of 30 ship-based and 8 drone-based videos is used to evaluate detection accuracy, horizon coverage, and computational performance. The results show that hybrid methods achieve the highest accuracy and most reliable horizon estimates. In contrast, purely classical methods exhibit reduced robustness, particularly in visually ambiguous scenes. Performance on ship-based imagery was consistently higher than on drone-based imagery, indicating a strong dependency on acquisition characteristics. The created dataset and codes used in this study are made publicly available to support further research on this topic. The code is available at https://github.com/allythe/HorizonDetection. The dataset is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20411867

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSB-GFM，一个用于跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架，通过多语义基元表示学习范式将多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合，并结合语义-结构双通道架构和域对抗训练实现有效的跨域知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型（GFMs）基于单标签假设，将多标签节点压缩为单个表示点，导致语义纠缠，难以同时区分多个标签；此外现有方法局限于同域场景，缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出多语义基元表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合；同时设计了语义-结构双通道架构，并引入域对抗训练实现跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验证明了所提模型在跨域多标签节点分类任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基元表示有效解决了多标签节点的语义纠缠问题，并通过双通道架构与域对抗训练实现了跨域泛化，为图基础模型在多标签场景下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [130] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4框架，一个基于成语的跨概念创造力评估框架，通过构建跨概念网络将目标槽位映射到可成像的替代概念，生成184个合成和37个人工构建的测试项目，在十种多模态大语言模型上进行评估，最强闭源模型仅达约50%的准确率，揭示了当前MLLM在创造性跨概念解码方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造力在设计、教育、人机协作等领域至关重要，但由于缺乏明确的奖励信号和评估目标，难以对其进行有效评估。跨概念理解是接收性创造力的核心认知能力，但目前缺乏系统化的评估方法。

Method: 提出了C4评估框架，将项目构建操作化为跨概念编码（将目标槽位沿人工标注并经第三方审核的跨概念网络中的桥梁路径映射到可成像替代概念），将模型推理操作化为跨概念解码。构建了C4-Eval数据集（184个合成+37个人工项目），每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化，共884个答案恢复案例。

Result: 在十种MLLM评估中，最强闭源模型主准确率仅达50.7%和48.0%，开源模型显著更低。候选约束可大幅提升准确率，但桥梁提示和解释请求改进有限。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码含义方面存在显著能力差距，C4框架为该能力的系统化评估提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [131] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种专为动态字节补丁设计的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构，用Top-K专家层替代密集前馈模块，利用补丁熵作为路由信号来组织稀疏计算，在字节级语言模型上取得最低的每字节比特数（bits-per-byte），同时保持相当的下游任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级大语言模型虽通过动态分组的字节补丁使无分词器建模更具竞争力，但所有补丁仍使用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据补丁语义和粒度的差异自适应调整模型容量。

Method: EntropyMoE将全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层；每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定工作负载贡献；路由器直接从补丁熵选择专家，补丁熵与长度共同构成特征空间来调节专家特化。

Result: 在匹配的密集和稀疏基线中，EntropyMoE实现了最低的留出法每字节比特数，同时保持了可比的下游准确率。

Conclusion: 补丁熵被证明是稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，EntropyMoE将MoE建模扩展到了基于分词器表示之外的领域。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [132] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对文本生成视频（T2V）技术催生的纯合成虚假新闻视频检测问题，提出新颖的三分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），构建首个纯合成虚假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，并提出推理引导的R-T2V框架，在10个基线模型上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V生成模型可从零合成虚假新闻视频，超越了以往拼凑现有素材的廉价伪造方式，形成了模态对齐陷阱，导致现有检测器失效；同时现有数据集缺乏纯合成伪造样本，直接提示T2V模型会产生单模态捷径和语义-视觉退化问题。

Method: 将T2V-FNVD形式化为三分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），构建PS-FNVD数据集（包含对齐欺骗的虚构事件Type 1和虚假视觉来源的真实事件Type 2），并提出R-T2V框架，通过条件化理由生成和监督微调，将高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹相结合来预测三分类真实性标签。

Result: 在10个主流基线上的大量实验表明，R-T2V达到最先进性能，准确率比第二名基线高出12.20个百分点，宏F1值高出8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: R-T2V框架有效解决了纯合成虚假新闻视频检测难题，通过融合语义推理与生成痕迹实现更鲁棒的检测，为T2V时代的虚假新闻治理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，用于稀疏MoE奖励模型的响应级可解释性分析。现有路由权重方法只能揭示专家接收了哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何评判响应。CoCo通过选择被采纳/被拒绝响应对中贡献差异最大的样本，联合捕获路由与偏好行为，实现更忠实、连贯且专业化的专家角色刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型是学习人类偏好中的核心组件，但其预测机制难以解释。稀疏MoE奖励模型虽然通过路由到专门专家提升可解释性，但路由权重仅反映专家"接收"了哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何"评判"响应，对专家行为的刻画不完整。

Method: 提出Contribution-Contrast（CoCo）响应级解释方法：使用贡献对比最大的被采纳-被拒绝响应对来表征专家角色，同时捕捉路由行为和偏好判断行为，实现对MoE奖励模型专家的忠实解释。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo相比基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法，生成了更连贯、更忠实且更专业化的专家解释，同时保持了具有竞争力的奖励建模准确性。这是首个针对MoE奖励模型解释方法的系统性研究。

Conclusion: CoCo成功弥补了传统路由权重解释方法的不足，为MoE奖励模型的专家角色提供了更全面的理解和更可信的解释框架，是该领域首个系统性研究，为奖励模型的可解释性开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [134] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo是一个基于记忆引导的动态证据探索框架，通过三层检索状态（文档模式记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情景记忆）结合贝叶斯页面信念更新、汤普森采样和空间邻近传播，实现长文档推理中的跨轮次动态证据定位，在3个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档理解系统受限于静态检索（固定top-k页面集合，无法从早期检索错误中恢复）和脆弱的跨轮记忆；近期迭代方法虽支持多轮证据获取，但未研究跨轮状态的传播机制，难以追踪页面相关性的动态变化。

Method: 提出DocMemo框架，维护三层检索状态：文档模式记忆（结构先验）、页面信念记忆（动态相关性估计）、问题情景记忆（查询特定推理轨迹）。通过贝叶斯页面信念更新与汤普森采样实现跨轮页面选择的连续精化，结合空间邻近传播和结构感知的自适应粒度证据访问，并以细粒度视觉区域补充页面级证据。

Result: 在3个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能，实验验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。

Conclusion: DocMemo通过显式建模跨轮状态传播机制和动态信念更新，有效解决了长文档推理中静态检索和脆弱记忆的局限，为长文档理解提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [135] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种基于大语言模型引导的可解释、免训练、无监督社区检测方法，受自然系统中相变动力学启发，采用四阶段流水线（初始化、合并、细化、选择），利用LLM归纳显式逻辑规则，在真实数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标驱动的社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，深度学习方法虽然性能更好但缺乏可解释性且依赖标注数据和训练过程。作者希望利用LLM强大的推理能力和世界知识来实现可解释、无需标签的社区检测。

Method: LUCID采用四阶段流水线：(1)局部视图社区初始化，使用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2)多因素社区合并，利用LLM归纳的规则迭代合并局部社区；(3)多粒度社区细化，应用LLM诱导的由粗到细规则并行减少边界噪声；(4)全局视图社区选择，基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为无监督方法达到了最先进性能，一贯优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID证明了LLM能够在社区检测中提供可解释且有效的无监督解决方案，通过将隐式知识转化为显式逻辑结构，在不依赖标注数据和训练过程的情况下实现优越性能。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests.
